### [1] [Surgical Video Generation From Diffusion to World Models: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26214)
*Fuxiang Huang,Chenxu Zhang,Liang Han,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2024-2026年手术视频生成领域的文献，将研究分为无条件生成、条件生成和世界模型生成三大类，指出该领域正从合成视觉上可信的帧向建模手术场景因果动态转变，并识别了泛化、物理真实感、可控性和可解释性等关键瓶颈，同时总结了公开数据集上的代表性方法实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术视频数据是术中感知、手术工作流理解和机器人决策模型的核心训练资源，但临床数据获取受限于隐私、成本和类别不平衡等问题。手术视频生成作为一种解决方案发展迅速，但缺乏清晰的概念框架，亟需系统性的梳理和总结。

Method: 对2024-2026年的手术视频生成文献进行调查，将现有方法划分为三类：无条件生成（unconditional generation）、条件生成（conditional generation）和世界模型生成（world modeling generation），并在公开数据集上对代表性方法进行了定量实验结果汇总。

Result: 揭示了手术视频生成任务定义的根本转变——从合成视觉上可信的帧转向建模手术场景的因果动态；识别了像素级保真度与临床合理性之间的持续差距；确定了泛化、物理真实感、可控性和可解释性四个关键瓶颈；提供了公开数据集上代表性方法的定量参考。

Conclusion: 该综述为手术视频生成领域提供了结构化的现状概览和开放挑战总结，为智能感知、多模态融合、生成式AI和手术数据科学交叉领域的研究者提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Surgical video data provides the primary training resource for models of intraoperative perception, surgical workflow understanding, and robotic decision-making. However, clinical data acquisition remains constrained by privacy, cost, and class imbalance. Surgical video generation has emerged as a transformative approach to addressing data scarcity and as a foundation for surgical simulation, training, and robotic policy learning. The field has developed rapidly without a clear conceptual framework. This survey organizes the 2024-2026 literature into three categories: unconditional generation, conditional generation, and world modeling generation, revealing a fundamental shift in how the task is defined from synthesizing visually plausible frames to modeling the causal dynamics of surgical scenes. We examine the persistent gap between pixel-level fidelity and clinical plausibility, and identify generalization, physical realism, controllability, and interpretability as bottlenecks. We further summarize experimental results of representative methods on public datasets to provide a quantitative reference for the field. This survey provides a structured overview of the current state and open challenges, offering a reference for researchers working at the intersection of intelligent perception, multi-modal fusion, generative AI, and surgical data science.

</details>


### [2] [Procedura: Agentic 3D Modeling with Procedural Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26238)
*Youtian Lin,Yikang Yang,Zhanpeng Hu,Mengqi Zhou,Feihu Zhang,Xun Cao,Jiaheng Liu,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Procedura，一个基于LLM的3D建模代理框架，将3D形状表示为程序化装配（procedural assembly），通过参数化程序描述物体，包含命名部件和可机器检查的配合约束。从文本提示出发，代理先生成装配图，再逐部件编写程序，并通过编译、配合和连接性检查验证，最后由视觉批评者迭代优化，生成可编辑、带部件结构和材质及关节的3D模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的原生3D生成器虽然能从单张图像恢复精细的网格几何，但存在明显缺陷：密集网格在机械加工物体应有的尖锐边缘处是柔和的、缺乏部件分解、没有用户可编辑的参数。这些限制阻碍了3D生成结果在实际设计与制造场景中的应用价值。

Method: Procedura采用"3D形状即代码"的范式，利用LLM的编码能力。具体流程包括：(1) 从文本提示规划物体为装配图；(2) 逐部件编写程序，基于配合框架求解部件位置而非猜测；(3) 通过编译、配合和连接性检查后才接受部件；(4) 解耦的视觉批评者逐诊断地完善装配；(5) 装配图携带逐部件的材质信息和经模拟器验证的关节。

Result: 在P3D-Bench数据集的装配评判器下，以及在自建的MechBench-36硬表面基准上，Procedura在评判质量上均超越了最先进的原生3D生成器和所有先前的3D代码代理。此外，它生成了所有评估方法中最尖锐的边缘，并且是唯一输出可编辑、带部件结构程序的方法。

Conclusion: Procedura验证了将3D形状表示为程序化装配这一范式的可行性，通过将LLM的编码能力扩展到3D建模，不仅能生成高几何质量的模型（尤其在锐利边缘上优于原生生成器），还能提供部件级分解和可编辑参数，弥合了生成式3D建模与工程设计之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Native 3D generators now recover impressive mesh geometry from a single image. However, a dense mesh stays soft where a machined object should be sharp, it carries no part decomposition, and it exposes no parameter a user could edit. To address this, we explore the paradigm of 3D shape as code, leveraging and scaling the coding ability of an LLM for 3D modeling. We introduce Procedura, a novel 3D modeling agent framework that writes an object as a procedural assembly, a parametric program whose named parts are joined by typed, machine-checkable mates. From a text prompt, the agent plans the object as an assembly graph and writes the program part by part, solving each placement from the mated frames rather than guessing it, and admitting a part only once compile, mate, and connectivity checks pass. A decoupled vision critic then refines the assembly one diagnosed fix at a time. Moreover, the same graph carries per-part materials and a simulator-validated articulation. We evaluate on P3D-Bench under its assembly judge, and with the same judge on MechBench-36, our hard-surface benchmark. On both, Procedura outperforms state-of-the-art native 3D generators and every prior 3D-code agent on judged quality, produces the sharpest edges of any method we evaluate, and is the only one whose output is an editable, part-structured program.

</details>


### [3] [Modality Maturity Index: A benchmark for assessing multimodal capabilities of omni models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26317)
*Rohit Patel,Dieuwke Hupkes,Sloan Strader*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Modality Maturity Index (MMI)，一个跨五种模态（文本、图像、音频、视频、文档）及最多三种模态组合的多模态评估基准，包含893道自包含问题，并引入Modality Presence Score (MPS)作为主要评估指标。在五个前沿多模态模型上的实验显示MPS仅为15.6到34.9，表明现有模型在模态生成方面表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架几乎只关注双模态理解（通常是文本加另一种模态），无法全面衡量前沿模型声称的跨模态能力，亟需一个覆盖更多模态组合的评估基准。

Method: 构建MMI基准，包含893道精心设计的问题，覆盖五种模态及最多三种模态的输入输出组合；每个问题附带人类撰写的评分标准（rubrics），通过LLM裁判按标准评分计算MMI Value；同时引入Modality Presence Score (MPS)作为补充指标，评估模型是否生成预期模态（而非仅内容正确性）。

Result: 在五个前沿多模态模型上，MPS仅为15.6（Claude Opus 4.6）到34.9（GPT-5.4）。在附带定制生成工具的实验中，LLM裁判依据rubrics评分与盲评人类标注者在70.8%的判断上一致。

Conclusion: 前沿多模态模型在模态覆盖能力上仍有很大不足，MPS作为主要结果反映了模型模态生成能力的瓶颈；LLM裁判结合人工rubrics的评分方式具有一定可行性（70.8%一致率），为该方向的自动化评估提供了验证。

Abstract: Frontier language models are increasingly marketed as omni systems that can perceive and respond across modalities. Existing evaluation frameworks, however, focus almost exclusively on bimodal understanding, typically text plus one other modality. We propose the Modality Maturity Index (MMI), a benchmark designed to evaluate the multimodal capabilities of large language models across five modalities (text, image, audio, video and document) and combinations of up to three modalities in both inputs and outputs. MMI consists of 893 questions, each carefully crafted to require the model to demonstrate its understanding of multiple input modalities and to generate responses that incorporate various output formats. The questions are designed to be self-contained, with clear expectations for the correct modality or mix of modalities required for an accurate response. Every MMI prompt carries human-authored rubric criteria for each output modality expected in the response; a model's MMI Value expresses the average of the per-modality scores for each prompt. Because low scores can reflect either failure to generate a modality (lack of presence) or failure to generate correct content, we introduce also a supplementary Modality Presence Score (MPS), a per-prompt F1 over the expected output modalities. Applying MMI to five frontier multimodal models, we find that the MPS ranges from only 15.6 (Claude Opus 4.6) to 34.9 (GPT-5.4). Given the low availability of returned modalities to even grade, we report MPS as our main result pending model improvements. To assess the viability of judging output correctness with LLM judges and rubrics, we run a separate experiment with custom generation tools. On the assets that generates, we find that an LLM judge applying the rubrics agrees with rubric-blind human annotators (who score the outputs directly and never see the criteria) on 70.8% of judgments.

</details>


### [4] [Finding the Right Evidence: Factor-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Reasoning for Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26355)
*Baixuan Xu,Yinyui Xu,Tianshi Zheng,Zhaowei Wang,Weiqi Wang,Haochen Shi,Jiayu Liu,Qing Zong,Xiyu Ren,Xinyu Geng,Zhitao He,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了PACE框架，通过两阶段证据获取（先因子引导索引，后对比线索验证）解决长视频问答中证据稀疏且缺乏选项判别性的问题，在MMR-V等基准上优于直接推理和先前智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答中相关证据稀疏，且问题相关上下文往往无法提供区分正确选项与干扰项的线索；先前智能体系统虽提高了线索检索却未带来相应的答案准确率提升，瓶颈在于选项判别性证据而非主题相关性。

Method: PACE采用两阶段框架：第一阶段在不可见候选答案的情况下，由问题派生因子引导索引片段级描述；第二阶段利用候选答案推导对比性线索并查询索引进行验证。

Result: 在MMR-V上使用Qwen3-VL骨干达到42.6%准确率，优于直接推理和先前智能体基线（包括DVD）；在诊断子集上恢复了66.9%的标注线索；在LVBench、Video-MME、EgoSchema和LongVideoBench上均有持续增益。

Conclusion: 选项感知的证据获取策略具有跨基准的泛化能力，其性能提升与证据恢复能力的改善相关，而非仅依赖更强大的答案侧先验。

Abstract: While LVLMs rapidly improve, long-video question answering still remains challenging: relevant evidence is sparse, and question-relevant context often fails to provide cues that discriminate the correct answer from plausible alternatives. Diagnostic analysis on a manually annotated subset of MMR-V shows that prior agentic systems substantially improve cue retrieval over direct VLM inference yet fail to achieve a corresponding gain in answer accuracy, indicating that the bottleneck lies in option-discriminative evidence rather than topical relevance alone. We propose PACE (Progressive Acquisition of Critical Evidence), a factor-guided framework for long-video evidence acquisition. PACE proceeds in two stages: it first indexes clip-level descriptions guided by question-derived factors without observing the candidate answers; it then uses the candidate answers to derive contrastive cues and queries the index for verification. On MMR-V with the open-source Qwen3-VL backbone, PACE achieves 42.6% accuracy, outperforming direct inference and prior agentic baselines including Deep Video Discovery (DVD). On the same diagnostic subset, PACE recovers 66.9% of the annotated cues, providing empirical evidence that its gains are associated with improved evidence recovery rather than stronger answer-side priors alone. Consistent gains over DVD on LVBench, Video-MME, EgoSchema, and LongVideoBench suggest that option-aware evidence acquisition transfers beyond MMR-V. Code is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/PACE.

</details>


### [5] [A Unified Framework for the Mechanics of Information in Convolutional Neural Network Image Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26363)
*Aryan Shukla,Matthew Toews*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的数学框架，将CNN中的信息传播与物理空间中的相对论能量-动量关系联系起来，揭示了对称和反对称滤波器分别对应静能和动量项，并通过拉普拉斯驱动的结构与热方程、薛定谔方程和弗里德曼方程建立数学对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在连接物理空间与信息空间的描述，为CNN中的信息传播建立统一的数学框架，跨学科地解释卷积神经网络的深层结构原理。

Method: 通过数学对应关系，将离散滤波器的对称性（对称分量Σ=[1,1]和反对称分量∇=[-1,1]）与相对论能量-动量关系（静能mc²和动量项pc）联系起来，建立β参数与滤波器能量比率的类比，并利用拉普拉斯驱动结构连接热方程、薛定谔方程和弗里德曼方程。

Result: 在多种物理尺度的3D图像（有机分子、硅晶体、人脑MRI、星系和CMB）中验证了该方法，发现了类斑点、尺度不变的Morse临界点。

Conclusion: CNN中的信息传播与物理空间中的相对论性扩散存在深刻的数学同构性，该框架可能为理解深度学习与物理世界的关系提供新的视角。

Abstract: This paper introduces a unified mathematical framework for modeling information propagation through convolutional neural networks (CNNs), with the aim of connecting descriptions of physical space and information space.
  A correspondence is presented linking discrete filter symmetry and the relativistic energy--momentum relation under the widely used nonlinear rectified convolution operation. Specifically, symmetric filter components (e.g. the sum $Σ= [1,1]$) operate analogously to rest energy $mc^2$ in preserving the image centre of mass (e.g. isotropic diffusion), whereas antisymmetric components (e.g. the gradient $\nabla = [-1,1]$) operate analogously to the momentum term $pc$ in generally inducing a displacement (e.g. vibration or translation). For typical small discrete filters, this displacement is determined by the ratio of antisymmetric to total filter energy, analogously to how the displacement of a relativistic particle relates to a Lorentz transform with beta parameter $β= \frac{v}{c}=\frac{pc}{E}$ equal to the ratio of momentum $pc$ to total energy $E$.
  Repeated filtering leads to the Gaussian scale-space and emergent scale-invariant features. These constructions share a Laplacian-driven structure with the classical heat (diffusion) equation and, via standard mathematical correspondences, with the Schrödinger equation and aspects of the Friedmann equations, together with emergent Morse topological structure. Demonstrations in 3D images reveal blob-like, scale-invariant Morse critical points in images spanning a wide range of physical scales, including organic sugar molecules and inorganic silicon crystals, human and primate brains in magnetic resonance images (MRI), galaxies and the cosmic microwave background (CMB).

</details>


### [6] [VIPER: An Expert-Curated Benchmark for Vision-Language Models in Veterinary Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26382)
*Luca L. Weishaupt,Simone de Brot,Javier Asin,Llorenç Grau-Roma,Nic G. Reitsam,Andrew H. Song,Dongmin Bang,Stefan T. Kaluziak,Long Phi Le,Jakob Nikolas Kather,Faisal Mahmood,Guillaume Jaume*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VIPER，这是首个面向毒理学病理学的视觉语言模型评估基准，包含1,251个问题、419张H&E染色大鼠组织学图像，覆盖七个器官系统。通过对16个模型的基准测试（包括新引入的兽医病理学模型、人类病理学专业模型和通用前沿模型），揭示了兽医与人类病理学之间存在显著的领域差距，暴露了前沿模型对正常组织过度诊断的风险，并表明领域特定训练对视觉基础预测至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的病理学视觉语言模型基准集中在人类组织（尤其是肿瘤学）上，忽略了非人类病理学领域，特别是在临床前药物安全性评估中扮演核心角色的毒理学病理学（实验室动物微观组织检查），因此需要一个专门针对该领域的评估基准。

Method: 构建了VIPER基准，包含419张H&E染色大鼠组织学图像、1,251个问题（涵盖多项选择、KPrim和自由文本格式），覆盖七个器官系统，所有问题均由委员会认证的兽医病理学家策划和验证。在此基础上对16个模型进行基准测试，包括2个新引入的兽医病理学模型、7个人类病理学专业模型和7个通用前沿模型。

Result: 基准测试结果显示了兽医病理学与人类病理学之间存在显著的领域差距，揭示了前沿模型对正常组织存在过度诊断的风险，并证明领域特定训练对于视觉基础预测仍然至关重要。

Conclusion: VIPER作为第一个毒理学病理学基准，填补了非人类病理学评估的空白，为视觉语言模型在药物安全性评估领域的应用提供了重要评估工具。数据和评估代码已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Pathology vision-language models are advancing rapidly, yet existing benchmarks remain focused on human tissue, particularly oncology, leaving non-human pathology largely unaddressed. This gap is especially important in toxicologic pathology, where microscopic tissue examination of laboratory animals is a core component of preclinical drug safety assessment. To address it, we introduce VIPER, the first expert-curated benchmark for vision-language model evaluation in toxicologic pathology. VIPER contains 1,251 questions associated with 419 H&E-stained rat histology images across seven organ systems, covering multiple-choice, KPrim, and free-text formats. All questions were curated and validated by board-certified veterinary pathologists. In total, we benchmarked 16 models, including two newly introduced veterinary-pathology models, seven human pathology-specialized models, and seven general-purpose frontier models. The results identify a substantial domain gap between veterinary and human pathology, expose the risk of over-diagnosis of normal tissue in frontier models, and show that domain-specific training remains critical for visually grounded predictions. VIPER data and evaluation code are available at https://github.com/mahmoodlab/viper.

</details>


### [7] [Mapping Woody Vegetation from Multi-Source Imagery and Prediction Fusion for Enhanced Data Efficiency and Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26471)
*Kal Backman,Jared Wood,Adam Roff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，通过数据融合技术提升深度学习模型在树木覆盖制图中的数据效率和鲁棒性。方法包括图像合成归一化去除影像缺陷（错误减少38.2%）、预测融合减少对单一影像质量的依赖（错误减少53.6%），以及利用标签迁移多源图像作为数据增强手段（错误减少28.1%-76.2%，性能标准差降低13倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树木覆盖制图依赖高质量无云无缺陷的遥感影像，而深度学习模型需要大量人工标注数据，且对影像质量变化敏感。现有方法在数据效率和鲁棒性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出一个包含三种方法的框架：(1) 图像合成方法，对影像进行归一化并移除缺陷；(2) 预测融合方法，减少对单一影像质量的依赖；(3) 标签迁移方法，将标注从多个影像源进行迁移作为数据增强，提高数据效率。

Result: 图像合成和预测融合分别将错误减少38.2%和53.6%；多源影像学习将错误减少28.1%-76.2%，并将不同影像日期性能的标准差降低13倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据融合框架能显著提升树木覆盖分割模型对影像质量变化的鲁棒性，同时通过多源标签迁移有效提高数据效率，其中多源学习带来的性能提升最为显著。

Abstract: Tree cover maps are a fundamental remote sensing product, used to derive ecological insights about the landscape and are essential to change detection, vegetation mapping and fire monitoring programs. However, comprehensive tree cover mapping requires reliable and high-quality imagery, free of cloud and weather defects to ensure accurate model outputs. Deep learning approaches can generate high quality maps with minimal human intervention but require large amounts of human annotated data to be successful. In this work we propose a framework consisting of methods that aim to improve the data efficiency and robustness of deep learning models using data fusion techniques to segment woody vegetation defined as vegetation over the height of 2m across the state of New South Wales, Australia. To improve robustness against varying image quality, we propose an image composition method that normalizes the imagery and removes defects, whilst also minimizing the reliance on individual image quality by proposing a prediction fusion method. The two methods resulted in an error reduction of 38.2% and 53.6% respectively compared to single-source imagery. To address deep learning approaches' limitation of requiring large amounts of data, we apply label transfer to multiple sources of imagery as a form of data augmentation to improve data efficiency. Learning from multiple image sources was shown to be the biggest improvement in performance, resulting in an error reduction between 28.1% to 76.2% across the different validation experiments, whilst reducing the standard deviation of performance across image dates by a factor of 13.

</details>


### [8] [Zero-Shot Video Restoration and Enhancement with Text-to-Image Latent Diffusion Models and Multi-Modal References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26476)
*Cong Cao,Huanjing Yue,Xin Liu,Jingyu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于文本到图像潜在扩散模型的零样本视频修复框架，通过双提示调谐反转与采样、纹理感知视频令牌合并等技术，在显著提升推理速度的同时增强了视频的时间一致性和修复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本图像修复方法（基于文本到图像潜在扩散模型）在应用于视频修复时会产生严重的时间闪烁问题，缺乏有效的视频级时间一致性约束。

Method: 提出了双提示调谐反转与采样来降低推理时间至原来的近1/3；提出纹理感知视频令牌合并（texture-aware video token merging）来充分利用帧间时间相关性；进一步提出参考自注意力（referenced self-attention）和参考令牌合并（referenced token merging）以支持图像参考。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在恢复和增强时间一致性视频方面具有优越性，推理时间大幅缩短，性能和时序一致性均显著增强。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架实现了零样本视频修复与增强，在保证高效推理的同时获得了良好的时间一致性和修复效果。

Abstract: Zero-shot image restoration methods with text-to-image latent diffusion models have achieved great success in universal image restoration tasks without training. However, applying them to video restoration will result in severe temporal flickering. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for zero-shot video restoration and enhancement which uses a text-to-image latent diffusion model and multi-modal references. Through the proposed dual prompt tuning inversion and sampling, the inference time can be reduced to nearly 1/3 of the original. The performance and temporal consistency can be also significantly stregthened. By using the proposed texture-aware video token merging, the temporal correlation between frames can be further utilized to improve the temporal consistency. We futher propose the referenced self-attention and referenced token merging to support image reference. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method in restoring and enhancing temporally consistent videos.

</details>


### [9] [Learning Woody Clearing With Loss Alignment for Zero-Shot Regrowth and Woody Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26489)
*Kal Backman,Jared Wood,Adam Roff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双时相Sentinel-2影像的树木覆盖变化检测方法，通过引入损失缩放系数α来对齐模型目标与最终用户指标，并利用图像增强和生成技术实现模型对再生检测和树木分割任务的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型检测树木覆盖变化时，由于损失函数定义与最终用户指标不一致，生成的产品可能无法满足用户需求；同时，对于空间上稀有且模糊的事件（如植被再生检测），难以获取大规模标注数据集。

Method: 使用澳大利亚新南威尔士州7年的年度双时相Sentinel-2影像训练树木覆盖变化检测模型；引入损失缩放系数α以优化特定Fβ分数；提出输入图像增强和生成技术，包括激活最大化和基于手工特征的图像生成（镶嵌清除斑块和人工树木），实现零样本迁移到再生检测和树木分割任务。

Result: 引入α使精确率提升1.85倍或召回率提升1.12倍；树木分割任务相比先前研究将总体误差降低最高18.2%；零样本再生检测达到0.845的F1分数。

Conclusion: 通过损失缩放对齐模型目标用户指标，并结合图像生成增强技术，可以有效提升树木覆盖变化检测性能，并为零样本迁移到再生检测和分割任务奠定基础。

Abstract: Detecting woody clearing is vital for managing biodiversity. Deep learning models can detect change in woody vegetation from bitemporal remote sensing imagery, however generated products may not meet end-user specifications due to unaligned loss definitions. Further limitations of deep learning models are the reliance on large datasets which can be difficult to attain for spatially rare and ambiguous events such as regrowth detection. In this work we train a model to detect woody change using bitemporal Sentinel-2 imagery consisting of 7 years' worth of annual imagery across the state of New South Wales, Australia. To align the objective of the model with end-user metrics, we introduce the loss scaling coefficient $α$ which transforms the objective to optimize for specific $F_β$ scores. Introducing $α$ was found to increase precision by 1.85x or recall by 1.12x. We propose input imagery augmentation and generation techniques that allow the woody change detection model to zero-shot transfer to regrowth and woody segmentation tasks. For woody segmentation, image generation techniques using activation maximization with low $α$ values for stability and image generation techniques derived from handcrafted features utilizing a mosaic of clearing patches and artificial trees for contextual grounding were found to outperform prior woody segmentation works of the study area, reducing the overall error by up to 18.2%. For zero-shot woody regrowth, creating pseudo-post and prior images resulted in the model achieving an F1 score of 0.845, creating a foundation for future regrowth detection work.

</details>


### [10] [Video-FLAIR: Not Whether to Reason, But How](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26495)
*Yogesh Kulkarni,Pooyan Fazli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Video-FLAIR是一个针对视频多模态问答的训练框架，通过强化学习让模型为每个查询自适应选择不同的推理模式（感知推理、组合推理、深思推理），在提升准确率的同时大幅减少token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对所有查询采用统一的推理策略，导致简单任务上计算冗余、复杂任务上推理不足。不同类型的多模态查询需要不同的推理方式（感知、组合或深思），因此需要一种能够自适应选择推理模式的机制。

Method: 训练阶段，模型对同一提示在三种推理模式下生成响应并直接比较。设计了一个复合奖励函数，基于正确性、grounding和成本来评估和选择最有效的响应，同时抑制无依据或错位的深思推理。通过强化学习产生自适应推理的监督信号，无需逐查询的人工标注。

Result: 相比Qwen2.5-VL基线，Video-FLAIR在MathVista上提升+5.4，Video-Holmes上提升+4.8，Video-MMMU上提升+4.8。平均token使用量降至95，而always-thinking基线为417。

Conclusion: Video-FLAIR通过自适应推理模式选择，在不牺牲准确率的前提下显著降低了推理成本，验证了多模态问答中按需分配推理资源的有效性和可行性。

Abstract: Multimodal queries can require different types of reasoning. Some can be answered via perceptual reasoning, extracting information directly from the visual signal, while others require compositional reasoning that combines observations or deliberative reasoning that evaluates competing hypotheses. However, many existing methods apply a uniform reasoning strategy across queries, leading to unnecessary computation on simple tasks and insufficient reasoning on complex ones. We introduce Video-FLAIR, a training framework that learns to select the appropriate reasoning mode for each query using reinforcement learning. During training, the model generates responses under all three modes for the same prompt, enabling direct comparison. A composite reward compares these responses to favor the most effective one based on correctness, grounding, and cost, while discouraging unsupported or misaligned deliberation. This yields a supervision signal for learning adaptive reasoning without per-query annotations. Video-FLAIR improves accuracy over the Qwen2.5-VL base model by +5.4 on MathVista, +4.8 on Video-Holmes, and +4.8 on Video-MMMU, while reducing average token usage to 95 compared to 417 for always-thinking baselines.

</details>


### [11] [Systematic Literature Review of Machine Learning Models and Applications for Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26500)
*Nuzhat Khan,Ab Al-Hadi Ab Rahman,Shahriyar Masud Rizvi,Ibrahim Yousef Alshareef,Muhammad Nadzir Marsono,Muhammad Paend Bakht,Mohd Shahrizal Rusli,Shahidatul Sadiah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文基于PRISMA指南，系统回顾了过去十年（2015年1月至2025年1月）97篇OCR研究文献，梳理了AI模型从传统方法向深度学习的演进过程，分析了OCR在应用领域、数据类型、语言覆盖和挑战方面的变迁，并提出了自监督学习、多模态AI、AutoML、TinyML等未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OCR技术在过去十年取得了显著进步，但缺乏对其发展历程的系统性、全面性评估，尤其是对AI模型架构演进、多语言处理能力以及当前未解决挑战的综合分析仍十分有限。

Method: 遵循PRISMA系统综述指南，对2015年1月至2025年1月间发表的97篇OCR研究进行系统性文献回顾，分析AI模型演进、应用领域、数据类型、语言覆盖范围和挑战，并对关键OCR模型的性能、优势与局限性进行评估。

Result: 识别出OCR技术在结构化与非结构化文本、场景文本识别和多语言处理方面的重要演进；发现未解决的挑战包括低资源语言数据集不足、手写文本高变异性、字符视觉相似性以及实时OCR应用的约束；提出了包括自监督学习、多模态AI、AutoML、AI辅助后处理、TinyML和联合语料库建设等针对性解决方案。

Conclusion: 该综述为OCR领域提供了系统性的知识基础，提出的未来研究方向旨在提升OCR准确性并解决实时工业应用中的关键挑战，将指导后续研究与实践。

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) for text recognition using machine vision has significantly improved, particularly when handling heterogeneous textual data. Traditional OCR models struggle with script variations, writing styles, and degraded documents. Advancements in technology are leading to new AI models with improved architecture for handling multiple languages and complex data formats. Despite this progress, a comprehensive evaluation of OCR advancements remains limited. Based on the established preferred reporting items for systematic reviews and meta-analysis (PRISMA) guidelines, this literature review presents an extensive assessment of OCR research to trace the evolution of AI models over the past decade. It explores the transition in AI models, application domains, data types, linguistic coverage, and challenges. Through a detailed analysis of 97 selected studies published during January 2015 - January 2025, key OCR models are identified, and their performance, strengths, and limitations are analyzed. The findings highlight how OCR technologies have evolved to address structured and unstructured text, scene text recognition, and multilingual processing. Unresolved challenges include limited resources for underrepresented languages, high variability in handwritten text, visual similarity among characters, and constraints in real-time OCR applications. To address these issues, several promising approaches are proposed. Key suggestions include self-supervised learning, multimodal AI, automated machine learning (AutoML), AI-assisted postprocessing, tiny machine learning (TinyML), and the creation of joint corpora for script matching. The future recommendations aim to enhance OCR accuracy and tackle the challenges identified for real-time industrial applications. This study will guide future research and establish a foundation for OCR field.

</details>


### [12] [NeuDonatello: Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Accurate Neural SDF Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26504)
*Alvin Jinsung Choi,Wanhee Kim,Taeyun Kim,Dasol Hong,Wooju Lee,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuDonatello提出了一种通过建模和利用SDF不确定性来改进神经表面重建的框架，采用蒙特卡洛采样策略建模空间变化的不确定性，并引入自适应正则化和不确定性感知的尺度参数，仅使用多视角RGB图像即可实现最先进的表面重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表面重建仅依赖RGB图像时，由于无纹理区域、遮挡和场景固有的模糊性，SDF的估计往往存在不确定性，而现有方法通常忽略这些不确定性，导致重建表面不精确。

Method: 1) 使用蒙特卡洛采样策略建模空间变化的SDF不确定性；2) 基于不确定性开发自适应正则化，在RGB监督不可靠处强化几何约束；3) 引入不确定性感知的尺度参数用于SDF到密度的转换，实现空间变化密度的更精确建模。

Result: 大量实验表明，NeuDonatello在仅使用带姿态的RGB图像的情况下，在多种场景中实现了最先进的表面重建精度，并展现出稳健的性能。

Conclusion: 建模并利用SDF不确定性可以有效解决RGB-only表面重建中因场景模糊性导致的几何不准确问题，为神经表面重建提供了一个新的有前景的方向。

Abstract: Neural surface reconstruction has emerged as a powerful paradigm for recovering high-quality 3D surfaces from multi-view images. However, recovering accurate geometry solely from RGB images remains challenging due to uncertainties arising from textureless regions, occlusions, and inherent scene ambiguities. Existing methods often overlook such uncertainties, leading to inaccurate estimates of the signed distance function (SDF). We introduce NeuDonatello, a novel framework that models and leverages SDF uncertainty to improve surface reconstruction. Central to our approach is to model spatially varying uncertainty using a Monte Carlo sampling strategy. Using this uncertainty, we develop an adaptive regularization that selectively strengthens geometric constraints where RGB supervision is unreliable, avoiding incorrect surface reconstruction. We further introduce an uncertainty-aware scale parameter for the SDF-to-density conversion. Conditioned on uncertainty, this design enables more accurate modeling of spatially varying densities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NeuDonatello achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction accuracy, with robust performance across diverse scenes using only posed RGB images.

</details>


### [13] [HUG-VIS: A Multimodal Benchmark for Human-centered Understanding and Generation in Visual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26517)
*Fei Ma,Zebang Cheng,Minghui Li,Hongbo Xu,Yuyong Tan,Yihua Shao,Hanling Wang,Zhou Liu,Yuqing Gao,Dong Wang,Long Ma,Laizhong Cui,Nicu Sebe,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了HUG-VIS，一个面向以人为中心视觉智能的统一基准，包含30位专业演员的8,400段半身视频，同步提供视频、音频、文本和alpha抠像数据，覆盖情感识别、视频生成、语音克隆和视频抠像四项任务，并采用统一的零样本协议进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉智能资源是任务特定的，各任务（情感识别、视频生成、语音克隆、视频抠像）的数据集互不共享，缺乏一个统一基准来协调多模态信号的感知与生成，限制了多模态信号利用和更广泛的研究。

Method: 构建包含8,400段受控半身视频的统一基准，每段视频由专业演员按照280种情绪-动作-提示组合表演，同步采集视频、音频、文本和alpha matte。采用统一零样本协议，结合自动指标、标准特定平均意见评分和跨任务分析，评估多种开源和闭源模型。

Result: 研究发现：(i)情感识别中语言内容占主导，纯视觉情感识别最弱；(ii)视频生成和语音克隆中自动指标与人工判断总体一致但排名有所不同；(iii)运动下的边界保真度仍是人物抠像的主要障碍；(iv)任务难度随情绪、模型和指标而变化，且存在显著的跨任务相关性。

Conclusion: HUG-VIS填补了以人为中心视觉智能缺乏统一基准的空白，提供了多任务、多模态的共享资源，并揭示了当前模型在各项任务中的关键瓶颈与跨任务关联，为后续研究提供了参考。

Abstract: Visual intelligence seeks to perceive, interpret, and synthesize the visual world and is central to modern computer vision. Human-centered visual intelligence is especially demanding because it studies people as expressive, socially situated subjects whose meaning is rarely conveyed by appearance alone. It couples vision with audio and language across four representative tasks: human emotion recognition, human video generation, human voice cloning, and human video matting. Yet existing resources remain task-specific, providing modalities and annotations for individual problems rather than a shared foundation coordinating understanding and generation. This limits multimodal signal use and broader research. We address this gap with HUG-VIS, a unified benchmark for Human-centered Understanding and Generation in Visual Intelligence. It contains 8,400 seated half-body videos of 30 professional actors, each performing the same 280 emotion-action-prompt assignments under a controlled Mandarin studio protocol, with synchronized video, audio, text, and alpha mattes. We evaluate diverse open- and closed-source models across the four tasks under a unified zero-shot protocol using automatic metrics, criterion-specific mean opinion scores, and multiple cross-task analyses. Results show that (i) linguistic content dominates current emotion recognition, while purely visual affect recognition is weakest; (ii) in video generation and voice cloning, automatic metrics and human judgment agree overall but differ in their top rankings, requiring joint reporting; (iii) boundary fidelity under motion is the main remaining obstacle for human matting; and (iv) task difficulty varies across emotions, models, and metrics, with notable cross-task correlations. The dataset and results are available at https://github.com/GML-MMGroup/HUG-VIS.

</details>


### [14] [FAN-LoRA: A Fourier-Adaptive Nonlinear Low-Rank Adaptor for Medical Foundation Model Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26531)
*Ziquan Liu,Zhewei Zhu,Xuyang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FAN-LoRA，一种频域解耦的医学图像分割微调架构。该方法通过B样条驱动的低通分支处理全局结构对齐，结合离散傅里叶高通分支补偿局部纹理细节，有效缓解了SAM在医学图像跨模态和跨中心分布偏移下的性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM等视觉基础模型在自然图像分割上表现卓越，但直接迁移到医学影像面临严重的领域鸿沟（跨模态、跨中心分布偏移）。现有PEFT方法在严重分布偏移下性能退化，其根本原因是共享低秩子空间中异质频率成分的隐式纠缠，导致结构对齐不佳和边界模糊。

Method: 提出Fourier-Adaptive Nonlinear Low-Rank Adaptor (FAN-LoRA)，采用频域解耦的微调架构：通过B-spline驱动的低通分支实现全局结构对齐，与离散傅里叶高通分支协同配合进行局部纹理补偿，显式分离优化空间。

Result: 在三个跨模态和跨中心基准上的大量实验表明，FAN-LoRA持续优于最先进的PEFT基线方法，相比最强竞争者，在平均Dice分数上取得一致性提升，并显著减少边界误差，同时保持紧凑的模块大小且不牺牲计算效率。

Conclusion: FAN-LoRA通过频域解耦有效解决了SAM在医学图像微调中的分布偏移问题，验证了显式分离高低频优化空间对跨域医学分割任务的有效性，在性能、边界精度和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: The advent of vision foundation models, notably the Segment Anything Model (SAM), has catalyzed significant advancements in natural image segmentation. However, their direct transfer to medical imaging remains severely bottlenecked by profound domain gaps, such as cross-modality and cross-center shifts. Existing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) methods facilitate the adaptation of SAM to medical domains; nevertheless, they frequently suffer from performance degradation under severe distribution shifts. This vulnerability primarily stems from the implicit entanglement of heterogeneous frequency components within a shared low-rank subspace, which directly exacerbates sub-optimal structural alignment and localized boundary blurring. To overcome this representational bottleneck, we propose the Fourier-Adaptive Nonlinear Low-Rank Adaptor (FAN-LoRA), a novel frequency-decoupled fine-tuning architecture. FAN-LoRA explicitly separates the optimization space by employing a B-spline-driven low-pass branch for global structural alignment, synergistically coupled with a discrete Fourier high-pass branch for local textural compensation. Extensive experiments across three challenging cross-modality and cross-center benchmarks demonstrate that FAN-LoRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art PEFT baselines. Compared to the strongest competitors, our method achieves consistent improvements in average Dice scores and notable reductions in boundary errors, while maintaining a compact module size without compromising computational efficiency.

</details>


### [15] [Visual Information-Guided Parallel Decoding for Diffusion Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26580)
*Insu Lee,Wooje Park,Wonseok Shin,Jinwoo Son,Byonghyo Shim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉信息引导采样器（VIG-Sampler），用于改进扩散多模态大语言模型（dMLLMs）中的逐步解码策略。VIG-Sampler根据令牌与图像标记的注意力来优先选择解码令牌，并对图像注意力分布相似的候选令牌施加惩罚，以增加解码子集的信息增益。在7个基准和3个开源dMLLM上的实验表明，该方法的性能优于Info-Gain采样器。\n\n在CoT分析中，推理过程用\\boxed{}包裹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散多模态大语言模型的解码顺序对最终输出质量至关重要。现有策略要么仅基于确定性度量（偏向高频训练数据中的令牌），要么考虑令牌对后续预测的影响但不显式考虑输入图像，缺乏对视觉信息的显式建模。

Method: 提出VIG-Sampler，核心思路是：(1) 根据令牌与图像令牌的注意力权重优先选择解码令牌；(2) 施加约束，惩罚图像注意力分布与已选令牌相似的候选令牌，从而最大化解码子集的信息增益。

Result: 在7个标题生成和VQA基准上使用3个开源dMLLM进行实验，VIG-Sampler在标题生成基准上平均超过Info-Gain采样器19.3个CIDEr点，同时在COCO Caption上仅用一半解码步骤就超越了它。

Conclusion: 显式利用图像注意力信息来指导dMLLM的令牌解码顺序可以有效提升生成质量，并且能在减少计算开销（更少解码步骤）的同时获得更好的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion multimodal large language models (dMLLMs) have recently emerged as a new decoding paradigm for multimodal generation. Starting from a fully masked sequence, dMLLMs progressively decode the sequence by unmasking a subset of the remaining masked positions at each step. Since the selected tokens serve as the prediction context for subsequent steps, deciding which tokens to decode is crucial to the quality of the final output. The most common strategy prioritizes tokens based on a certainty measure that tends to favor tokens frequently observed in the training data. Recent approaches instead order tokens according to their influence on subsequent predictions, but do not explicitly account for the input image. We propose the Visual Information-Guided Sampler (VIG-Sampler), which prioritizes tokens based on their attention to image tokens. We further impose a constraint that penalizes candidate tokens whose image-attention distributions are similar to those of previously selected tokens, thereby increasing the information gain of the decoded subset. Extensive experiments on 7 captioning and VQA benchmarks with 3 open-source dMLLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of VIG-Sampler, which outperforms the Info-Gain Sampler by an average of 19.3 CIDEr points across the captioning benchmarks and surpasses it on COCO Caption while using only half as many decoding steps.

</details>


### [16] [DPA-I2P: Depth-Guided Projective Alignment for Image-to-Point-Cloud Registration in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26589)
*Wenxin Zhang,Hang Li,Zhiwei Xu,Qiankun Dong,Gang Wang,Tao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPA-I2P方法，通过深度引导的投影对齐来解决图像与点云配准中的跨模态对应学习难题，在KITTI和nuScenes数据集上显著提升了配准精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到点云配准是自动驾驶和室外定位中的基础任务。现有隐式对应学习方法虽已提升配准性能，但由于图像与稀疏LiDAR点云之间存在固有的模态差异，可靠的跨模态对应学习仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出三个核心模块：(1) Ray-Conditioned Metric Depth Encoding (RMDE) 和 (2) Projection-Consistent Vision Lifting (PVL) 以结构化、几何感知的方式利用深度和视觉线索；(3) Cross-Modal Query Pruning (CQP) 在早期细化阶段抑制不可靠的查询以提升匹配稳定性。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上，DPA-I2P相比最强隐式基线在RTE和RRE上分别降低45.0%和55.6%；在nuScenes数据集上也优于所有基线，展现出对不同驾驶场景的良好迁移性。

Conclusion: 深度引导的投影对齐方法有效解决了跨模态对应学习中的模态差异问题，显著提升了图像到点云配准的精度和稳定性，并在不同自动驾驶场景中表现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Image-to-Point Cloud Registration aims to estimate the camera pose of a given image within a 3D scene point cloud, which is a fundamental task in autonomous driving and large-scale outdoor localization. Recent implicit correspondence learning methods have improved registration performance by learning cross-modal alignment in an end-to-end framework, leading to more accurate camera pose estimation. However, due to the inherent modality discrepancy between images and sparse LiDAR point clouds, reliable cross-modal correspondence learning remains challenging. To address this issue, we propose Depth-Guided Projective Alignment for Image-to-Point-Cloud Registration (DPA-I2P). Unlike naive depth or feature concatenation, Ray-Conditioned Metric Depth Encoding (RMDE) and Projection-Consistent Vision Lifting (PVL) exploit depth and visual cues in a structured, geometry-aware manner. In addition, Cross-Modal Query Pruning (CQP) suppresses unreliable queries during early refinement to improve matching stability. Experiments on KITTI and nuScenes demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. On KITTI, DPA-I2P reduces RTE and RRE by 45.0% and 55.6% over the strongest implicit baseline, respectively. On nuScenes, DPA-I2P also improves registration accuracy over the evaluated baselines, suggesting better transferability to different driving scenes.

</details>


### [17] [FU-Mamba: A Frequency-Enhanced Dynamic Scanning Framework for Oralscan Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26607)
*Xinxin Zhao,Jinpeng Ye,Bo Wei,Liqin Wu,Mahmoud Hassaballah,Karen Egiazarian,Aura Conci,Victor Hugo C. de Albuquerque,Abdulkadir Sengur,Leszek Rutkowski,Yan Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FU-Mamba框架，通过动态扫描（Dynamic Mamba Block）和频域增强（frequency domain enhancement）改进口腔扫描图像分割性能，在牙科分割数据集上mIoU提升1.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉状态空间模型依赖手动设计的扫描顺序，破坏了语义空间连续性；同时数据采集中的光照不均、反光表面和噪声会扰乱频率分布（削弱高频细节、增强低频成分），阻碍边界准确定位。

Method: FU-Mamba包含两个核心组件：(1) 动态Mamba块（DMB），通过可训练的偏移预测网络自适应学习采样偏移并执行灵活的双线性插值，实现内容感知的扫描以保持空间连贯性；(2) 频域增强块，通过小波引导的分解和频谱池化平衡频谱成分，提升在不利成像条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在牙科分割数据集上，FU-Mamba的mIoU提升了1.1%，取得了显著的语义分割精度提升。

Conclusion: FU-Mamba通过结合动态内容感知扫描与频域增强，有效解决了口腔扫描图像分割中语义连续性破坏和频域失衡的问题，为数字牙科中的计算机辅助诊断与治疗规划提供了更精准的分割方案。

Abstract: Oralscan image segmentation is essential for computer-aided diagnosis and treatment planning in digital dentistry. However, existing visual state space models (SSMs) often rely on manually designed scanning orders to flatten image patches into sequences, which disrupts the semantic spatial continuity and hinders coherent feature extraction from key foreground regions. Moreover, elements such as inconsistent lighting, reflective surfaces, and noise during data acquisition disrupt the frequency distribution by diminishing high-frequency details while enhancing low-frequency components, consequently hindering the accurate localization of boundaries. In response to these challenges, we introduce FU-Mamba, an innovative framework that incorporates dynamic scanning and frequency domain enhancement within the SSM architecture. Specifically, the Dynamic Mamba Block (DMB) adaptively learns sampling offsets via a trainable offset prediction network and performs flexible bilinear interpolation, enabling content-aware scanning that preserves spatial coherence. Furthermore, a frequency domain enhancement block balances spectral components through wavelet-guided decomposition and spectrum pooling, improving robustness under adverse imaging conditions. Experimental findings indicate that FU-Mamba attains a notable enhancement in segmentation accuracy, evidenced by a 1.1% increase in the mean intersection over union (mIoU) metric when evaluated on the dental segmentation dataset. Project page: https://byte2bite.github.io/FU-Mamba/

</details>


### [18] [Text-to-seed generation: Training-free open-vocabulary seeded semantic segmentation via re-purposing diffusion as text-guided seed generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26624)
*Kumju Jo,Heesun Jung,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Text-to-Seed (T2S)，一个无需训练的开放词汇语义分割框架，利用Stable Diffusion的文本-区域对应关系生成注意力种子点，再将这些稀疏种子作为点提示输入SAM进行区域扩展，从而获得完整的物体掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放词汇语义分割方法通常用SAM来精炼其他模型产生的粗糙掩码，但当初始掩码不准确时这种策略不可靠。作者认为可以利用SAM作为基于准确物体种子点的区域扩展模块，而非基于不准确的粗糙掩码。

Method: 将开放词汇语义分割重构为两个步骤：1) 文本引导的种子定位——利用Stable Diffusion的文本到区域对应关系生成注意力种子点；2) 基于种子的区域扩展——将这些稀疏种子作为SAM的点提示，生成完整的物体掩码。整个框架无需训练和额外标注。

Result: 在标准开放词汇语义分割基准上取得了强性能，验证了将语义定位与种子驱动的空间分割相结合的 effectiveness。

Conclusion: 通过将SAM从粗糙掩码的"精炼器"转变为基于种子点的"扩展器"，并利用Stable Diffusion的语义定位能力，T2S在无需训练的情况下实现了可靠的开放词汇语义分割。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) aims to segment image regions corresponding to arbitrary text queries. Although the Segment Anything Model (SAM) is a powerful foundation model for segmentation, its standalone performance on OVSS remains limited. Existing methods therefore often use SAM to refine coarse masks predicted by other models, but this strategy is unreliable when the initial masks are inaccurate. In this work, we argue that more reliable segmentation can be achieved by exploiting SAM as a region expansion module guided by accurate object points (i.e., seeds) rather than inaccurate coarse masks. Inspired by classical seeded segmentation, we reformulate OVSS as text-guided seed localization followed by seed-based region expansion. To realize this idea, we propose Text-to-Seed (T2S), a training-free framework that leverages the text-to-region correspondence of Stable Diffusion to generate attention-based seed points for target categories described by text. These sparse seeds are then used as point prompts for SAM to produce full object masks. Without task-specific training or additional annotations, T2S achieves strong performance on standard OVSS benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining semantic grounding with seed-driven spatial segmentation.

</details>


### [19] [Who Remains, What Changes: Identity Anchored Composed Gait Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26632)
*Jingchen Fei,Zengbin Wang,Yukun Liu,Muyi Sun,Shibiao Xu,Man Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出组合步态检索（CoGR）新任务，通过参考序列和自然语言修改查询来检索目标步态序列。作者设计了基于大视觉语言模型的自动化标注流程，构建了首批步态-语言数据集，并提出了身份锚定的组合检索框架ComposeGait，通过部分感知身份适配器（PIA）防止身份漂移，在两个基准上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步态识别方法局限于刚性视觉匹配，忽略了自然语言指令在交互式检索中的潜力，缺乏将参考序列与语言修改查询结合的组合检索能力。

Method: 提出CoGR任务和ComposeGait框架。ComposeGait采用身份锚定的组合检索框架，其核心是部分感知身份适配器（PIA），通过聚合多帧、部位感知的身份证据生成样本特定的ID令牌，注入共享Q-Former的两个分支以保持身份一致性，且ID令牌输出不用于最终检索嵌入；同时联合身份与任务适应的组合检索目标进行端到端优化。

Result: ComposeGait在Language-Augmented CCPG上达到72.38%的R@1，在Language-Augmented CASIA-B上达到83.61%的R@1，均为最佳性能，建立了CoGR的强基线。

Conclusion: ComposeGait验证了组合步态检索任务的可行性，确立了该任务的强基线，数据集和代码将公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Gait recognition has achieved remarkable progress, yet existing methods remain confined to rigid visual matching and often overlook the potential of natural language instructions for interactive retrieval. In this paper, we introduce Composed Gait Retrieval (CoGR), a novel task that retrieves a target gait sequence based on a reference sequence and a natural language modification query. To address the absence of existing datasets for this task, we design an automated annotation pipeline powered by large vision-language models (VLMs) to construct the first gait-language datasets: Language-Augmented CCPG and Language-Augmented CASIA-B. Building on this, we propose ComposeGait, an identity-anchored composition framework designed to prevent the identity drift that arises when generic composed retrieval follows the instruction but returns the wrong person. Its Part-aware Identity Adapter (PIA) aggregates multi-frame, part-aware identity evidence into a sample-specific ID token. We inject the ID tokens into both branches of a shared Q-Former to preserve identity, while excluding the ID-token outputs from the final retrieval embeddings. Joint identity and task-adapted composed-retrieval objectives optimize this space end to end. We evaluate ComposeGait on both benchmarks and show that it achieves the best R@1 among the compared methods, reaching 72.38% on Language-Augmented CCPG and 83.61% on Language-Augmented CASIA-B. These results establish ComposeGait as a strong baseline for CoGR. The datasets and code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [20] [Real-time Unsupervised Object Discovery from Asynchronous Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26644)
*Pratham G. Shenwai,Hemant Kumar Singh,Sridhar Ravi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量级、无需训练的事件相机移动目标发现框架，通过线性时间的时空概率事件滤波器（SPEF）和事件Morton码聚类模块（EMCC），在保持高精度的同时大幅降低计算复杂度，适用于延迟敏感和资源受限的视觉感知场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机产生高稀疏异步数据流，在延迟关键的环境中需要高效发现移动目标，但现有的聚类方法依赖昂贵的距离矩阵计算，限制了实时应用。

Method: 框架包含两个核心模块：(1) SPEF，一种线性时间的时空概率事件滤波器，引入自适应事件接受阈值来区分显著运动结构与背景噪声；(2) EMCC，利用事件Morton码编码绕过距离矩阵计算，高效地对事件进行分组以实现无监督的移动目标发现。

Result: 在E-MLB数据集中，SPEF在经典滤波方法中达到最佳去噪性能，且与学习方法相当而无需离线训练；在对象发现任务中，EMCC在FRED和eTraM数据集上取得最高整体精度和最低执行时间，大幅超越密度聚类基线。

Conclusion: 该工作为事件数据中的经典目标发现建立了新的性能基准，提供了一种高度可扩展、无需训练的资源受限视觉感知解决方案。

Abstract: Event cameras capture pixel-level intensity changes with microsecond resolution to produce highly sparse asynchronous data streams. For visual perception in latency-critical environments, we propose a lightweight, training-free framework for discovery of moving objects based on spatio-temporal clustering. This framework is driven by two core contributions. First, a linear-time Spatio-temporal Probabilistic Event Filter (SPEF) that introduces an adaptive event acceptance threshold to distinguish salient motion structures from background noise. Second, an Event Morton Code Clustering (EMCC) module that bypasses expensive distance matrix computation to efficiently group events for unsupervised discovery of moving objects. On the E-MLB dataset benchmark, SPEF achieves the best denoising performance among classical filtering methods and remains competitive with learning-based approaches without requiring any offline training. On object discovery, EMCC achieves the highest overall accuracy and lowest execution time across the FRED and eTraM datasets, outperforming established density-based clustering baselines by a substantial margin. Overall, this work establishes a new performance benchmark for classical object discovery in event data, providing a highly scalable, training-free solution for resource-constrained visual perception. The code is available at https://github.com/PrathamShenwai/SPEF_EMCC

</details>


### [21] [Tissue-Mixture Entropy-Weighted Reconstruction for Partial-Volume-Aware Brain MRI Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26647)
*Xiao Tong,Wenyun Yang,Ziheng Zhang,Jingzhi Han,Zhaochu Luo,Jinbo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AGW-PBR方法用于脑部MRI超分辨率重建，通过组织混合熵加权来强调受部分容积效应影响的组织过渡区域，仅使用低分辨率图像即可推理，在多尺度IXI和fastMRI数据集上验证了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图像目标函数在脑部MRI超分辨率中会低估组织过渡区域，因为部分容积效应影响的区域仅占图像小部分，且二值边界无法准确捕捉体素内脑脊液、灰质和白质的连续混合比例。

Method: AGW-PBR结合LR-only重建骨干网络与训练时目标函数：骨干网络集成Sobel梯度引导、软潜基分配和有界网格锚定残差变形；将配准的T1/T2/PD IXI组织分数转换为组织混合熵，定义均值归一化的重建权重（仅训练时使用，推理仅需LR图像）。

Result: AGW-PBR在IXI数据集2x/4x/6x尺度上提升全图像重建质量，4x时组织界面区域保真度改善；无PVE监督的骨干在fastMRI 4x上保持强性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持使用组织混合熵加权来实现脑部MRI超分辨率的PVE感知重建。

Abstract: Full-image objectives in brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) super-resolution (SR) can underweight tissue-transition regions affected by the partial-volume effect (PVE), as these regions occupy only a small fraction of the image. Binary boundaries also do not capture the continuous mixture of cerebrospinal fluid, gray matter, and white matter within a voxel. We propose Anatomy-Guided Gaussian-Parameter Warping with PVE-Balanced Reconstruction (AGW-PBR), which combines a low-resolution (LR)-only reconstruction backbone with a training-time objective that emphasizes tissue transitions. The backbone integrates LR-derived Sobel guidance, soft latent-basis assignment, and bounded grid-anchored residual warping. Fixed, quality-controlled tissue fractions derived from registered T1/T2/PD IXI images are converted into tissue-mixture entropy, which defines mean-normalized reconstruction weights within validated PVE support. These sidecars are used only during training, and inference requires only the LR image. AGW-PBR is evaluated on T2-weighted IXI images at 2x, 4x, and 6x using three seeds and subject-level paired analyses. At 4x, test-only SynthSeg masks independently assess reconstruction in tissue-interface and non-interface regions. Targeted ablations examine valid-support supervision, spatially aligned entropy weighting, and soft latent assignment. The AGW-backbone is also trained from scratch on fastMRI at 4x without PVE supervision. AGW-PBR improves full-image reconstruction across the tested IXI scales and regional fidelity at 4x, while the PVE-free backbone retains strong performance on fastMRI. These findings support tissue-mixture entropy weighting for partial-volume-aware brain MRI SR.

</details>


### [22] [Hierarchical Channel Stacking: A Structured Decision Framework for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26648)
*Saifullah Shoaib,Akash Borigi,Rupendra Lekkala,Amaury Lendasse,Edward Ratner,Sai Sowjanya Bhamidipati,Alexander Schlager,Peggy Lindner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出分层通道堆叠（HCS）框架，将CNN中间激活转换为跨三个骨干阶段的60维结构化表示，用于AI生成图像检测。在GAN和扩散生成器的基准上达到86.7%准确率和宏F1分数，并通过阶段消融和贡献分析展示了分层结构的信息互补性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成图像检测器虽然预测准确，但难以提供关于决策形成过程的洞察。作者希望构建一个紧凑且可解释的检测框架，不仅能区分真实与合成图像，还能揭示模型如何跨不同表示层级整合证据。

Method: HCS框架包含三个部分：(1) 将三个CNN骨干阶段的中间激活转换为60维结构化表示；(2) 使用逐通道的Level-1分类器进行底层判断；(3) 通过Level-2聚合器生成图像级预测，同时保留显式分层结构用于可解释性分析。

Result: 在涵盖GAN和扩散生成器的基准上，HCS的留出测试集准确率达86.7%，宏F1为86.7%。阶段消融实验表明完整的三阶段系统优于单阶段和两阶段变体；阶段级贡献分析进一步显示伪造GAN和扩散图像具有不同的阶段贡献特征。

Conclusion: HCS不仅是一个紧凑的检测器，更是一个结构化框架，可用于研究合成图像检测器如何跨表示层级组装证据，为理解模型决策过程提供新的分析工具。

Abstract: Many synthetic-image detectors produce accurate predictions but offer limited insight into how those decisions are formed. This paper introduces Hierarchical Channel Stacking (HCS), a compact framework for AI-generated image detection that converts intermediate CNN activations into a structured 60-dimensional representation organized across three progressively deeper backbone stages. HCS uses per-channel Level-1 classifiers and a Level-2 aggregator to produce image-level predictions while preserving explicit hierarchical structure for analysis. On a benchmark spanning GAN and diffusion generators, HCS achieves 86.7% accuracy and 86.7% macro-F1 on the held-out test set. Stage ablation shows that the full three-stage system outperforms reduced single-stage and two-stage variants, indicating that the hierarchy carries complementary predictive information. Stage-level contribution analysis further shows that, in the analyzed detector setting, fake GAN and fake diffusion images exhibit distinct stage-level contribution profiles. These results position HCS not simply as a compact detector, but as a structured framework for studying how synthetic-image detectors assemble evidence across representation levels.

</details>


### [23] [CoGeo-GS: Concept-Driven and Geometry-Aware Multi-Object Removal in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26656)
*Yuanxiang Ni,Xianliang Huang,Chenhang Ma,Chen Xiao,Yuewen Ma,Ruxin Wang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoGeo-GS，一个基于概念驱动的3D场景多物体移除框架。通过为高斯赋予概念感知语义标签实现灵活物体选择与单阶段优化，并引入结合单目深度先验与扩散精化的几何补全流程，以及几何正则化的精化策略，在多物体移除任务中取得了优于现有方法的视觉质量与重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景中多物体移除面临严重遮挡、语义纠缠以及保持几何和多视角一致性的挑战。现有3DGS方法在单物体编辑中表现良好，但在多物体场景下扩展性差，常需要重复优化且移除区域几何不稳定。

Method: 1) 为高斯分配概念感知语义标签，实现灵活物体选择并减少前景物体与背景结构间的干扰，在单一优化阶段内完成；2) 提出结合单目深度先验、扩散精化和边界对齐混合的几何感知补全流程以恢复合理几何；3) 引入几何正则化精化策略增强重建稳定性并保持多视角一致性。

Result: 实验表明CoGeo-GS在视觉质量和重建保真度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CoGeo-GS通过概念驱动的方式有效解决了3D场景多物体移除中的遮挡、语义纠缠和几何一致性问题，验证了语义标签与几何补全流程相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-object removal in 3D scenes is challenging due to severe occlusions, semantic entanglement, and the difficulty of maintaining geometric and multi-view consistency. Existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods perform well for single-object editing but scale poorly to multi-object scenarios, often requiring repetitive optimization and yielding unstable geometry in removed regions. We propose CoGeo-GS, a concept-driven framework for controllable multi-object removal in 3D scenes. CoGeo-GS assigns concept-aware semantic tags to Gaussians, enabling flexible object selection and reducing interference between foreground objects and background structures within a single optimization stage. To recover plausible geometry, we introduce a geometry-aware completion pipeline that combines monocular depth priors with diffusion-based refinement and boundary-aligned blending. A geometry-regularized refinement strategy further stabilizes reconstruction and preserves multi-view consistency. Experiments demonstrate that CoGeo-GS outperforms existing methods in visual quality and reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [24] [PailitaoGR: Latent Think-with-Images for Generative Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26658)
*Xiaomeng Fan,Yueran Liu,Shengyu Zhou,Chenghan Fu,Wanxian Guan,Feng Li,Chuan Yu,Jian Xu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PailitaoGR，一种"潜在思考与图像"的生成式图像检索方法，通过目标聚焦感知机制和选择性辅助证据利用机制，使生成式检索模型能够在不裁剪的情况下聚焦搜索目标、在不依赖OCR的情况下读取图像信息，在真实在线图像搜索日志数据上平均超越现有基线13.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将生成式检索范式扩展到图像搜索具有挑战性，因为真实世界的查询图像包含多样信息（搜索目标、有用辅助证据、无关视觉内容），模型需要识别并聚焦搜索目标，同时选择性利用辅助证据。

Method: 提出Latent Think-with-Images方法，包含两大机制：(1)目标聚焦感知机制——通过目标增强器和基于on-policy蒸馏与注意力引导损失的学习策略，增强搜索目标区域的视觉token；(2)选择性辅助证据利用机制——通过辅助增强器和能力内增量对比蒸馏策略，增强并利用辅助证据的视觉token。训练和验证数据来自真实在线图像搜索日志。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在真实在线图像搜索日志构建的数据集上，平均超越现有基线13.8%，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PailitaoGR通过将目标聚焦感知和选择性辅助证据利用内化到生成式检索模型中，实现了"无需裁剪的缩放"和"无需OCR的阅读"能力，有效解决了真实图像搜索中信息多样性的挑战。

Abstract: Generative retrieval has demonstrated strong performance by directly generating product semantic identifiers (SIDs).
  Extending this paradigm to image search, however, is nontrivial because real-world query images contain diverse information, including the search target, useful auxiliary evidence, and irrelevant visual content.
  This requires the model to identify and focus on the search target while selectively utilizing auxiliary evidence. In this paper, we propose \textbf{PailitaoGR}, a \emph{Latent Think-with-Images} method for generative image retrieval, which internalizes target-focused perception and selective auxiliary-evidence utilization into a the generative retrieval model, enabling \textit{Zooming without Cropping} and \textit{Reading without OCR}. Specifically, we design a target-focused perception mechanism that identifies and enhances visual tokens of the search target, consisting of a target Enhancer and a learning strategy based on on-policy distillation and attention guidance loss, enabling the model to focus on search-target regions. We also design a selective auxiliary-evidence utilization mechanism that identifies and enhances visual tokens of auxiliary evidence, including an auxiliary enhancer and an in-capacity incremental contrastive distillation strategy, enabling the model to exploit auxiliary evidence. We construct training and validation sets sampled from real-world online image-search logs. Experiments show that our method outperforms existing baselines by an average of 13.8\%, validating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [25] [Hull First, Wake Second: Wake-Reliance Suppression for Robust Maritime Vessel Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26665)
*Yefan Wang,Xingyu Wang,Ruibiao Zhu,Yusen Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HullWake框架，采用"船体优先、尾流其次"的策略解决海事船只检测中因依赖尾流而导致的漏检和误检问题，并引入面向尾流的评测协议和Curated-Wake数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事船只检测中，船只船体往往较小、低对比度或模糊，而尾流更长更易检测，导致检测器过度依赖尾流信号——这可能漏检尾流弱或静止的船只，也可能在类似尾流的水面杂波上产生误检。

Method: HullWake采用船体优先、尾流其次的框架：将提案中心的船体证据与方向性尾流上下文分离，通过双向提案锚定走廊提取尾流线索，并通过尾流响应监督、尾流衰减一致性、仅尾流置信度抑制和船体-尾流去相关来抑制尾流主导的预测。同时引入面向尾流的评测协议和Curated-Wake数据集（约10,000张图像，新增检测和分割级别的尾流标注）。

Result: 与仅框检测器和掩码监督分割基线相比，HullWake在总体AP、弱/无尾流鲁棒性、类尾流误检、最差组AP以及尾流衰减后的置信度稳定性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: HullWake通过解耦船体与尾流证据并施加多重尾流抑制机制，有效缓解了海事船只检测中的尾流依赖问题，提升了检测的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Abstract: Maritime vessel detectors often face scenes where hulls are small, low-contrast, or blurred, while wakes are longer and easier to detect. This creates a wake-reliance problem: detectors may miss slow or stationary vessels with weak wakes, or produce false positives on wake-like water clutter. We propose HullWake, a hull-first wake-second framework for robust maritime vessel detection. HullWake separates proposal-centered hull evidence from directional wake context, extracts wake cues with bidirectional proposal-anchored corridors, and suppresses wake-dominant predictions through wake response supervision, wake-attenuated consistency, wake-only confidence suppression, and hull--wake decorrelation. We also introduce a wake-oriented evaluation protocol covering weak/no-wake vessels, wake-like hard negatives, worst-group AP, and confidence drop after wake attenuation. Experiments are conducted on Curated-Wake, a wake-oriented maritime dataset of about 10,000 images curated from Ships/Vessels in Aerial Images, the SMD benchmark, and SeaDronesSee, with newly added detection- and segmentation-level wake annotations. Compared with box-only detectors and mask-supervised segmentation baselines, HullWake improves overall AP, weak/no-wake robustness, wake-like false positives, worst-group AP, and confidence stability after wake attenuation.

</details>


### [26] [RECAP-Forcing: Retaining Content Appearances for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26671)
*Haiyang Xu,Zheng Ding,Zhuowen Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RECAP-Forcing，一种基于外观新颖性而非时间邻近性来组织记忆的训练免费推理方法，用于长自回归视频生成中的KV缓存管理，通过注意力汇聚和基于光流的新颖性库，在无额外可学习参数的情况下持续提升多种强基线的视觉质量和语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长自回归视频生成面临根本性记忆挑战：在有限的注意力窗口内，模型必须决定从不断扩展的历史中保留哪些信息。现有方法按时间组织记忆（优先保留近期帧并压缩或丢弃旧帧），忽略了视频本质上是由不断演化的主体、物体和场景组成的，这些元素的身份一致性需要跨时间维持。

Method: 提出按外观新颖性组织记忆的框架：1）在视频开头，所有可见内容都是新颖的，通过注意力汇聚保留初始场景；2）随着视频演进，基于光流的新颖性库通过选择性保留新揭示的内容（如进入的主体、去遮挡区域、新引入的场景）来扩展相同原则；3）当新内容首次出现时保留其KV缓存，优先考虑新颖性而非最近性。

Result: 作为无需训练、无额外可学习参数的推理方法，RECAP-Forcing在多个强基线上持续改善视觉质量和语义保真度，并优于现有的记忆方法。

Conclusion: 记忆应该随新引入内容的数量扩展而非视频长度扩展，这种外观索引的记忆使长距离一致性成为记忆结构的显式属性，为长视频生成中的记忆管理提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Long autoregressive video generation faces a fundamental memory challenge: with a finite attention window, a model must decide which information from an ever-expanding history to retain. Existing methods organize memory temporally, preserving recent frames while compressing or discarding older ones. We instead propose RECAP-Forcing, organizing memory by appearance novelty. A long video is not merely a sequence of frames, but an evolving cast of subjects, objects, and scenes whose identities must remain consistent over time. We organize memory by retaining the KV cache associated with newly appearing content--such as entering subjects, disoccluded regions, and newly introduced scenes--at the moment it first becomes visible, prioritizing novelty over recency. Memory should scale with the amount of newly introduced content, rather than with video length. This appearance-indexed memory makes long-range consistency an explicit property of the memory structure. Our framework unifies two mechanisms under this single principle. At the beginning of a video, when all visible content is novel, an attention sink preserves the initial scene. As the video evolves, an optical-flow-based novelty bank extends the same principle by selectively retaining newly revealed content. As a training-free inference method with no additional learnable parameters, RECAP-Forcing consistently improves visual quality and semantic fidelity across multiple strong baselines and outperforms existing memory methods.

</details>


### [27] [Reason in the Words You Speak: Idiolectal Paraphrasing Off-Policy Traces for Reasoning Distillation in VideoLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26684)
*Ji Soo Lee,Jinyoung Park,Seohyun Lee,Jongha Kim,Joonmyung Choi,Jinsung Yoon,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Echo-GRPO框架，通过将教师模型的推理轨迹改写成学生策略自身的"个人语体"（idiolect），解决GRPO中on-policy与off-policy分布不匹配导致的推理能力学习不足问题，并在视频推理蒸馏任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO的on-policy特性限制模型只能从自身已能产生的推理技能中学习，而直接注入教师模型的off-policy推理轨迹会因分布不匹配导致梯度裁剪，使得模型只学会正确答案而未真正学到支撑答案的推理过程。

Method: 提出Echo-GRPO框架，核心思想是让模型用"自己的话"进行推理：将教师模型的高概率但低概率的推理轨迹通过Dual-Reference Decoding改写成学生策略的idiolect（特征词汇和表达模式），在保留语义的同时使其与学生策略分布对齐。具体实例化为VideoEcho-R1用于视频推理蒸馏。

Result: 在三个多模态LLM骨干和五个基准上取得一致改进，并且验证了idiolect改写是一个即插即用模块，能同时提升RL和SFT框架下的推理蒸馏效果。

Conclusion: 策略对齐的监督信号不仅限于GRPO，还可扩展到更广泛的推理蒸馏框架。让模型用自己熟悉的语言表达推理过程，比强迫其模仿教师的表达方式更能有效学习高级推理能力。

Abstract: Recent large language models achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, where reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has emerged as a leading paradigm for optimizing models on self-generated trajectories. However, the on-policy nature of GRPO bounds the model to the reasoning skills it can already produce, restricting to learn more advanced capabilities. Prior works inject privileged reasoning traces from a stronger teacher policy to guide training, yet these traces are inherently out of distribution with respect to the student policy. We observe that this mismatch between on-policy and off-policy causes gradient clipping on semantically critical reasoning tokens, ultimately rewarding correct answers while leaving the reasoning that justifies them unlearned. Hence, we propose \textbf{Echo-GRPO}, a framework that lets the model reason in the words it speaks. Rather than imitating low-probability privileged traces from the teacher model, Echo-GRPO rewrites them into the student policy's own \textit{idiolect}, that is, its own characteristic vocabulary and expression patterns, while preserving their semantics via Dual-Reference Decoding. We instantiate this framework as \textbf{VideoEcho-R1} for video reasoning distillation, achieving consistent improvements across three multimodal LLM backbones and five benchmarks. Finally, we show that our idiolectal paraphrasing is a plug-in module that consistently improves both RL and supervised fine-tuning frameworks for reasoning distillation, demonstrating that policy-aligned supervision extends beyond GRPO.

</details>


### [28] [Domain-Specific Self-Supervised Representation Learning for Retinal Fundus Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26686)
*Bekzat Nurlanbekova,Fung Fung Ting*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在资源受限条件下，利用SimSiam和SimCLR两种对比自监督学习框架进行视网膜疾病分类，重点探讨了针对视网膜图像的增强策略和训练参数对表示学习的影响。实验表明，定制化的增强策略对性能提升至关重要，轻量级SSL框架即使在小批量、有限标注数据的条件下也能学习到可迁移的表征，获得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像标注数据稀缺且获取成本高昂，监督学习需要大量标注数据，而对比自监督学习为利用无标注数据学习有效表征提供了有前景的替代方案。

Method: 对比评估SimSiam和SimCLR两种SSL框架，在资源受限条件下结合视网膜特异性增强技术，使用小批量训练，并通过线性评估和微调在下游任务（多疾病分类和糖尿病视网膜病变分级）中评估表征质量。

Result: 针对视网膜图像特点定制的增强策略对性能提升起关键作用；在受限条件下，轻量级SSL框架仍能学习到可迁移的表征，减少对大规模标注数据的依赖并获得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 视网膜特定增强策略是SSL在医学图像上成功的关键因素，轻量级自监督学习框架在资源受限的医疗场景中具有实用可行性。

Abstract: Despite the growing number of public datasets, annotated medical images remain scarce. Supervised learning methods achieve strong performance on many benchmarks, however require large amounts of labeled data, which are costly and time-consuming to obtain in the medical domain. To address this limitation, contrastive self-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as a promising alternative for learning useful representations from unlabeled data. In this work, we investigate two SSL frameworks, SimSiam and SimCLR, for retinal disease classification from fundus images. We focus on understanding how augmentation strategies and training parameters influence representation learning under resource-constrained settings. Given limited data and computational capacity, we explore the feasibility of training SSL models with small batch sizes incorporated with retinal-specific augmentation techniques. Through a series of experiments, we assess the quality of learned representations via linear evaluation and fine-tuning across downstream tasks, including multi-disease classification and diabetic retinopathy grading. Our results show that tailoring augmentation strategies to the characteristics of retinal images plays a critical role in improving performance. Even under constrained settings, lightweight SSL frameworks can learn transferable representations that reduce dependence on large annotated datasets and achieve competitive results.

</details>


### [29] [AesCanvas: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Aesthetic Critique and Contextual Suitability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26713)
*Xuanwei Hu,Haoyu Dong,Kejun Wu,Tianyi Liu,Jianjun Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AesCanvas基准套件，包含CritiqueCanvas（54,300张图像上的519,136对指令-响应，支持多维度长文美学批评）和ContextCanvas（301个专家评审的使用场景，评估上下文美学适配性）。研究发现美学专业模型在批评生成指标上表现良好，但在情境判断上明显落后于通用MLLM，表明文化情境、证据支撑的适配性应作为美学建模的独立目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有美学评估基准主要衡量内在视觉质量或固定领域标准，未考虑图像对特定用途、受众、文化背景或领域惯例的适配性，缺乏统一的评估框架来考察MLLM在情境化美学判断方面的能力。

Method: 构建AesCanvas统一套件：CritiqueCanvas提供大规模多维度美学批评数据（覆盖摄影、绘画、虚拟图像），ContextCanvas提供专家评审的真实使用场景测试；在统一协议下评估闭源前沿、开源通用与美学专用MLLM，结合参考词汇与语义指标以及上下文分析来全面评测模型表现。

Result: 批评生成与情境判断之间存在明显分离：基于参考的词汇和语义指标只能部分捕捉批评质量；美学专用模型在部分批评指标上有竞争力，但在ContextCanvas上明显落后于强通用MLLM；美学专业化不能可靠地迁移到情境适配性，模型决策可能无法追踪或扎根于决定性的上下文视觉线索。

Conclusion: 文化情境化、证据支撑的适配性是美学建模中区别于传统美学评估的独立目标，为未来美学模型的设计与评估指明了新方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have extended Image Aesthetic Assessment (IAA) beyond scalar scores toward interpretable critique and guidance. Yet existing benchmarks mainly assess intrinsic visual quality or fixed domain criteria, leaving open whether an appealing image is appropriate for a specific purpose, audience, cultural setting, or domain convention. We introduce AesCanvas, a unified suite with two complementary components: CritiqueCanvas with 519,136 instruction-response pairs from 54,300 images supports long-form, multi-dimensional critique across photography, painting, and virtual imagery, whereas ContextCanvas with 301 expert-reviewed use scenarios evaluates contextual aesthetic suitability in realistic use scenarios. Under a unified protocol, we evaluate closed-source frontier, open-weight general, and aesthetic-specific MLLMs. Results reveal a clear separation between critique generation and context-sensitive judgment: reference-based lexical and semantic metrics only partially capture critique quality, while aesthetic specialists remain competitive on selected critique metrics yet substantially lag strong general-purpose MLLMs on ContextCanvas. Further analyses show that aesthetic specialization does not reliably transfer to contextual suitability and that model decisions may fail to track or ground themselves in decisive contextual visual cues. These findings establish culturally situated, evidence-grounded suitability as a distinct objective for aesthetic modeling.

</details>


### [30] [LiveVVT: High-Fidelity Video Virtual Try-On in Real Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26714)
*Yushe Cao,Shikun Feng,Ruxiang Duan,Liyong Wang,Dianxi Shi,Chun Yu,Junliang Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveVVT 是一个滚动流式扩散框架，用于视频虚拟试穿（VVT），通过在有界窗口内保持双向时空建模的同时实现因果循环生成，配合时间记忆和全局外观记忆维持长序列一致性，并通过渐进蒸馏框架对齐训练与推理，实现高保真实时流式VVT，较同类模型延迟降低26倍、吞吐量提升11倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的视频虚拟试穿依赖完整视频片段进行双向时空建模，导致在实际持续部署中产生极高的延迟和计算开销；而简单强制执行因果性会破坏预训练的双向先验，严重降低合成质量。因此需要一种能在因果循环生成中保持有界双向建模的高效流式方法。

Method: 提出LiveVVT框架，核心包括：（1）滚动流式扩散——在固定大小窗口内联合去噪多个视频块，通过有界前瞻保留局部双向交互，每次迭代输出一个干净块；（2）双记忆机制——有界时间记忆传播近期动态与遮挡上下文，持久全局外观记忆从目标服装和正面试穿关键帧构建以锚定服装细节；（3）渐进蒸馏框架——集成双向VVT学习、教师轨迹回归实现因果少步自适应，以及协作匹配蒸馏，将教师分布匹配与真实视频上的滚动流匹配相结合，使优化与循环推理对齐。

Result: 在成对和未成对的长序列基准上，LiveVVT取得了优于同规模模型的生成质量，同时延迟降低26倍、吞吐量提升11倍，实现了高保真度的实时流式视频虚拟试穿。

Conclusion: LiveVVT成功解决了扩散式VVT在持续流式部署中的效率和一致性难题，证明了有界双向建模与因果循环生成的结合可以在不牺牲生成质量的前提下实现实时流式虚拟试穿，为实际商业部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Video Virtual Try-On (VVT) achieves high visual fidelity through bidirectional spatio-temporal modeling, but complete-clip dependence incurs prohibitive latency and computational overhead in practical continuous deployment. Naively enforcing causality disrupts pretrained bidirectional priors and substantially degrades synthesis quality. We introduce LiveVVT, a rolling streaming diffusion framework that preserves bounded bidirectional modeling within causal recurrent generation. Within a fixed-size window, LiveVVT jointly denoises multiple video chunks under bounded look-ahead, preserving local bidirectional interactions while emitting one clean chunk per iteration. Beyond the window, two complementary memories sustain long-term consistency: a bounded temporal memory propagates recent dynamics and occlusion context, whereas a persistent global appearance memory, constructed once from the target garment and a frontal try-on keyframe, anchors garment details and dressed appearance throughout the stream. We further introduce a progressive distillation framework integrating bidirectional VVT learning, teacher-trajectory regression for causal few-step adaptation, and Collaborative Matching Distillation, which couples teacher-distribution matching with rolling flow matching on real videos to align optimization with recurrent inference. Experiments on paired and unpaired long-sequence benchmarks demonstrate superior generation quality over similarly sized models, with $26\times$ lower latency and $11\times$ higher throughput, enabling high-fidelity real-time streaming VVT.

</details>


### [31] [Beyond Atomic Layouts: Compositional Design Understanding with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26716)
*Yiyang Huang,Zhaowen Wang,Simon Jenni,Jing Shi,Yitian Zhang,Yizhou Wang,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出组合布局理解任务及CoDeLayout数据集（约2万个真实多层布局样本），并设计MASON后训练范式，通过多模态对齐和结构感知分别缓解语义漂移和结构模糊问题，使Qwen2.5-VL 7B达到91.66%准确率，超越GPT-o3的79.68%，且仅用30%训练数据即可超过全量直接微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）虽能很好地理解原子布局（由独立元素组成），但在需要推理视觉上缠绕元素和层级多结构关系的组合布局时表现不佳。作者旨在解决这一空白，并深入分析现有VLM在此任务上的关键不足。

Method: 提出MASON后训练范式，包含两个核心模块：多模态对齐（MA）将元数据定义的元素锚定到其视觉对应物，缓解语义漂移；结构感知（SP）建模层级感知的元素间空间关系，改善层级理解并减少结构模糊。同时构建了CoDeLayout VQA数据集用于评估。

Result: 强基线GPT-o3仅达79.68%准确率，而Qwen2.5-VL 7B配合MASON达到91.66%。MASON仅用30%训练数据即可超越全量直接微调（Direct Finetune），并且随数据增加展现出更好的扩展性。

Conclusion: 组合布局理解仍是现有VLM的重大挑战，MASON范式通过多模态对齐与结构感知的有效结合，显著提升了模型在此任务上的表现，且训练效率优于传统全量微调方法。

Abstract: Layout understanding, or the interpretation of element organization, is essential for document analysis, user interface (UI) creation, and graphic design. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) excel at interpreting atomic layouts composed of independent elements, they struggle with compositional layouts that require reasoning over visually entangled elements within hierarchical multi-layer structures. In this paper, we introduce a new task, compositional layout understanding, and present CoDeLayout, a VQA dataset of ~20K real-world multi-layer layouts annotated with compositional element pairs and design intent. Through empirical analysis on CoDeLayout, we identify two key challenges for existing VLMs: semantic drift between textual metadata and visual content, and structural ambiguity in hierarchical inter-element relationships. To address these challenges, we propose MASON, a post-training paradigm that integrates multimodal alignment (MA) and structural perception (SP). MA enhances element interpretation by grounding metadata-defined elements to their visual counterparts, mitigating semantic drift, while SP models layer-aware inter-element spatial relationships to improve hierarchical understanding and reduce structural ambiguity. Experiments reveal substantial gaps in existing VLMs: even the strongest baseline, GPT-o3, achieves only 79.68% accuracy, whereas Qwen2.5-VL 7B with MASON reaches 91.66%. Notably, MASON surpasses full-data Direct Finetune using only 30% of the training data and scales better with additional data.

</details>


### [32] [Parameter Efficient Continual Learning for Sparse Event-Based Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26720)
*Vaishnavi Nagabhushana,Kartikay Agrawal,Ayon Borthakur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出sLoTh，一个面向预训练稀疏事件视觉Transformer的参数高效持续学习框架。通过冻结骨干网络，仅更新不到1%的参数（seLoRA低秩注意力更新和共享神经元阈值调制），在无需回放缓冲区的情况下，在CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet等数据集上实现竞争性的类增量学习性能，同时能耗比传统稠密视觉Transformer降低约6.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人与边缘智能系统面临持续数据流，需要模型在不断适应新数据的同时保持已有知识，且受严格的存储和能耗约束。现有参数高效微调方法依赖稠密计算的视觉Transformer，部署成本高；稀疏事件视觉Transformer虽能显著降低能耗，但其持续学习能力尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: sLoTh采用冻结骨干网络策略，将可塑性限制在两部分：一是可扩展高效低秩注意力更新（seLoRA），二是共享神经元阈值调制。该方法无需经验回放缓冲区，仅更新少于1%的模型参数即可实现持续适应。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet、ImageNet-100和ImageNet-R上，最多100个任务的实验中，sLoTh在类增量学习和在线持续学习场景下取得了具有竞争力的无回放性能，同时能耗比传统稠密视觉Transformer低约6.5倍。

Conclusion: sLoTh证明了稀疏事件视觉Transformer结合参数高效持续学习策略，能够在保持极低能耗的同时实现无回放的持续学习，为边缘智能和机器人应用提供了可行的节能持续学习方案。

Abstract: Robotic and edge intelligence systems operate in dynamic environments where data arrives continuously, requiring models to adapt while preserving previously learned knowledge under strict memory and energy constraints. While parameter-efficient fine-tuning has shown promise for continual learning with vision transformers, conventional architectures rely on dense computation and remain costly for real-world deployment. Sparse event-based vision transformers provide energy-efficient event-driven computation, yet their continual learning capabilities remain largely unexplored. We here introduce sLoTh, a parameter-efficient continual learning framework for pretrained sparse event-based (spiking) vision transformers. sLoTh freezes the backbone and restricts plasticity to scalable-efficient low-rank attention updates (seLoRA) and shared neuronal threshold modulation, enabling adaptation without replay buffers by updating less than 1% of model parameters. Experiments across CIFAR-100, Tiny-ImageNet, ImageNet-100, and ImageNet-R with up to 100 tasks demonstrate competitive rehearsal-free performance in class-incremental learning and online continual learning, while enabling approximately 6.5x lower energy consumption than conventional dense vision transformers.

</details>


### [33] [UniGeo: A Multi-modal Large Language Model for Text-Guided Cross-View Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26722)
*Jiahao Wen,Hang Yu,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniGeo，一个统一的多模态大语言模型（MLLM），用于文本引导的无人机地理定位任务。UniGeo在共享视觉-语言框架上联合支持地理语义理解、跨视角语义生成和候选级验证，通过多阶段训练策略逐步学习这些能力，在GeoText-1652数据集上显著提升了定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将文本引导的无人机地理定位视为开放文本查询与候选图像的直接匹配，但面对不完整的查询描述和高相似度的候选图像，全局跨模态匹配难以实现可靠的细粒度定位。

Method: UniGeo构建在统一的多模态大语言模型框架上，包含三个核心能力：1）地理语义学习，建立局部场景要素、空间关系与语言描述之间的稳定对应；2）跨视角语义生成，建模无人机视角与卫星视角之间的语义映射；3）即插即用的验证模块，对高混淆候选进行细粒度判别。采用多阶段训练策略逐步学习上述三项能力。

Result: 在GeoText-1652数据集上，UniGeo相比现有方法在R@10和mAP上分别提升13.59和2.83个百分点，多个检索骨干网络上均表现出一致的性能改进。

Conclusion: UniGeo通过将地理语义理解、跨视角生成和候选验证相结合的统一框架，有效解决了文本引导无人机地理定位中因查询不完整和候选相似度高导致的细粒度定位难题。

Abstract: Text-guided drone geo-localization aims to identify a target region in a large-scale image gallery from a natural-language description. Existing methods mainly formulate this task as direct matching between an open-ended text query and candidate images. However, incomplete queries and highly similar candidates often make global cross-modal matching insufficient for reliable fine-grained localization. We propose UniGeo, a unified multimodal large language model (MLLM) for text-guided drone geo-localization. Built on a shared vision-language framework, UniGeo jointly supports geo-semantic understanding, cross-view semantic generation, and candidate-level verification. Specifically, it establishes stable correspondences among local scene elements, spatial relations, and language descriptions through geo-semantic learning, and further models semantic mappings between drone and satellite views through cross-view generation. Based on these capabilities, a plug-and-play verification module performs fine-grained discrimination among highly confusable candidates. We further introduce a multi-stage training strategy that progressively learns geo-semantic understanding, cross-view generation, and candidate verification, improving adaptation to text-guided geo-localization. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple retrieval backbones. On GeoText-1652, UniGeo improves R@10 and mAP by 13.59 and 2.83 percentage points, respectively, validating its effectiveness for fine-grained text-guided drone geo-localization.

</details>


### [34] [GeoMAD: Geometry-Aware Multi-View Anomaly Detection via Deformable Fusion and Distributional Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26724)
*Shang-Fu Chen,Jhih-Ciang Wu,Kuan-Chuan Peng,Wen-Huang Cheng,Kai-Lung Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoMAD，一个统一的多视角多类别异常检测框架，通过Cross-view Deformable Fusion Module（CDFM）实现内容自适应的跨视角几何对应，并结合Distributional View Alignment（DVA）对齐各视角的分布一致性，在不依赖相机标定、体素构建或类别特定3D监督的情况下，实现了高效且几何感知的跨视角融合。实验在Real-IAD和MANTA-Tiny上验证了其在检测和定位上的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角异常检测的核心挑战在于如何在保留几何感知的同时实现可扩展的多类别工业融合。现有方法存在两个极端：基于体素的融合提供了明确的几何对齐但需要昂贵的3D构建和类别特定假设；轻量级基于patch的融合虽然高效但依赖离散候选匹配，缺乏连续的跨视角对应。因此需要一种兼顾几何对应性和分布一致性的统一框架。

Method: GeoMAD包含两个核心模块：(1) Cross-view Deformable Fusion Module（CDFM）在2D特征图上学习内容自适应、视角对特定的采样偏移，并通过多尺度窗口金字塔和图像全局参考采样实现层次化跨视角对应，无需相机标定或3D监督；(2) Distributional View Alignment（DVA）是一种自监督的跨视角正则化损失，将每个视角的瓶颈分布对齐到逐实例的视角中心目标，强制执行全局一致性而不需要像素级对应。

Result: 在Real-IAD和MANTA-Tiny数据集上的大量实验表明，GeoMAD在统一的多视角异常检测任务中取得了优异的检测和定位性能。

Conclusion: GeoMAD通过CDFM和DVA分别桥接局部几何对应与全局分布一致性，提供了兼具几何感知和分布一致的融合方式，同时保留了2D特征空间学习的高效性，为统一的多视角多类别异常检测提供了一个有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-view anomaly detection (MvAD) detects defects by exploiting complementary observations from multiple camera viewpoints. The central challenge is to fuse views with sufficient geometric awareness while remaining scalable to multi-class industrial settings. Existing methods typically fall into two extremes: voxel-based fusion provides explicit geometric alignment but requires costly 3D construction and class-specific assumptions, whereas lightweight patch-based fusion is efficient but relies on discrete candidate matching and lacks continuous cross-view correspondence. In this paper, we propose GeoMAD, a unified multi-view, multi-class AD framework that addresses both geometric correspondence deficiency and distributional inconsistency. Our \textit{Cross-view Deformable Fusion Module} (CDFM) learns content-adaptive, view-pair-specific sampling offsets directly on 2D feature maps and arranges them across a multi-scale window pyramid with image-global reference sampling, enabling hierarchical cross-view correspondence without camera calibration, voxel construction, or class-specific 3D supervision. We further introduce \textit{Distributional View Alignment} (DVA), a self-supervised cross-view regularization loss that aligns each view's bottleneck distribution against a per-instance view-centric target, enforcing global consistency without pixel-level correspondence.
